# ADDITIONAL CONTEXT

<!-- Context: world_state.md -->

# World State — Out of the Abyss (the Ember Vanguard campaign)

*Current through Chapter 70 (played 2026-09-28). Present-tense sections describe the state at the end of ch70. Where sources conflict, the later chapter wins. Items marked **[Unconfirmed]** were not settled on tape and should not be treated as fact.*

*Provenance: synthesized from all 67 structured Markdown files in `docs/summaries/`, using the summary-native chronology and entity dossiers in `docs/ensemble_summary_prototype/summary_native/`.*

---

## 1. Current Situation (end of Ch70)

- **Where the party is:** In the hidden volcanic cavern deep beneath Candlekeep, at the summit of the **Obsidian Tower**. The party is inside or immediately outside the **Inner Book Chamber**, behind the now-open **Obsidian Door**.
- **What just happened:**
  - Zalthir carried the **second Manshoon simulacrum** through the anti-magic field and threw it into the **Lava Pit**.
  - **Edvaldo**, a doppelganger serving Manshoon, smashed **2 of Alaundo the Seer's 6 unplayed prophecy crystals**. Gyrgum secured the **4 survivors**.
  - Gyrgum, as the verified Reader, spoke the riddle answer ("A candle"). The door opened.
  - Daz used Mage Hand to reach the **Book of Vile Darkness** without touching it, and covered it with an illusion that it had turned to ash. He resisted the book's influence. The book offered him a choice of self-knowledge or knowledge of the demons, and he chose the demons. The book is now giving him information about Underdark events. Its trustworthiness is deeply suspect.
- **Immediate pressures:**
  - **Miirym's boon** widened the anti-magic field to 30 ft for 10 minutes. Six minutes had elapsed when ch70 began, so the boon is expiring or expired.
  - **Candlekeep's surviving The Avowed Readers** are expected to arrive shortly. They will not let artifacts of extraordinary power disappear unnoticed.
  - **Edvaldo** fled as a halfling toward the anti-magic barrier, along the only route Thorin was not blocking. His current whereabouts are unknown.
  - The **real Manshoon** is still at large. The simulacrum warned that killing it would bring the real Manshoon's vengeance.
  - **Hundreds of Glyphs of Warding** protect the tower's tomes. An explosion could trigger a catastrophic chain reaction.
- **Party level:** 8. The level-up happened in ch52, and no later level-up is recorded.
- **Party name:** The **the Ember Vanguard**. Earlier joke names include "Firefly Tactics" and "Ember Grapple". The name is notorious in Gracklstugh, where it is tied to wildly exaggerated legends, including a "20th-level drow archmage". Kalan Strongbranch has also deputized the party as **Watchers of Candlekeep**.

---

## 2. The Party (Player Characters)

### Thorin
- **Identity:** A dwarf raised by a hill giant tribe. He speaks Dwarvish with a Giant accent, speaks Giant, and wears giant clothes. He shares the giants' dislike of dragons. He defends his adoptive "different giants" against surface-dwarf prejudice from the War of the Giants.
- **Martial kit:** Martial combatant. Recorded abilities: Hill Strike (learned from a hill giant battle master), Action Surge, Menacing Attack (frightens), sapping strikes (target has disadvantage on its next attack), Sentinel, 10-ft blindsight, and Know Your Enemy. He took Sharpshooter at level 8 with a GM house rule putting the +1 into Strength. His attack bonus with Dawnbringer is +10. He carries darts and javelins.
- **Weapon:** **Dawnbringer**, a sentient sunblade (see §6). Their relationship is ongoing: "couples counseling" with Candlekeep scholars, and he insists it is "purely platonic".
- **Temperament:** Radical candor and transparency. He dislikes NDAs and faction games and wants to "be my faction". He once played a dice-driven "blurt" mechanic. He takes a stand on rights: oozes, elemental labor, unions.
- **Recent deeds:**
  - Killed Moziqodo (ch63) and The Zhentarim assassin (ch65).
  - Solved the vault cryptogram (ch67).
  - Fell through the staircase trap and was caught mid-air by Zalthir (ch67–68).
  - Struck and frightened Edvaldo, then held the stairs to protect the prophecy stones (ch70).
- **Ties:**
  - **Eldeth Feldrun Feldrun** swore that her clan will embrace him. Thorin admits he has "a thing" for her.
  - Alaundo the Seer's prophecy names a "giant-raised dwarf" (Thorin) to be summoned by deep dwarves to an ancient forge.

### Zalthir
- **Identity:** A brass dragonborn Shadow Monk from a secretive order that teaches silence and avoiding frontal conflict. Formative influences: Korrh Erann (flee drow pursuit) and "The Garden Shadow" (cutting dangerous flora). The Medusa Neheedra Duskryn mocked "the circumstances of his birth", which hints at a stigmatized lineage. **[Unconfirmed — nature unknown]**
- **Capabilities:**
  - Darkness, which he can see through.
  - Shadow Step.
  - Flurry of blows and Stunning Strike.
  - Fire breath weapon.
  - Wings, which let him fly and hover.
  - Two tattoos. The Eldritch Claw / Empowered tattoo extends inky tendrils that strike and grapple at 15 ft.
  - Grappler build: Tavern Brawler and Street Justice (restrain with rope).
  - Minor Illusion.
- **Gear:** A Potion of Flying, stolen from Alkrist's safe and stored inside Glabbagool.
- **Training:** Passed Candlekeep's Trial of the Broken Mirror (Brother Khell-Vire-Vire / Master Kenshi), though he failed every skill demonstration and redeemed himself with a strike. He is mentor to **Glabbagool**, his formal shadow-monk apprentice and sidekick.
- **Obligations:** Owes **Spanner** a study session of his "mechanical symmetry", alongside Glabbagool. This is collateral for the borrowed modron tools.
- **Recent deeds:**
  - Destroyed the first Manshoon simulacrum (ch69).
  - Bound Edvaldo (ch69).
  - Grappled the second simulacrum and threw it into the lava (ch70). He accepted the risk that this might destroy the prophecy crystals.
  - Earlier: killed Asha Vandree Vandree (ch53) and The Pudding King (ch47).
- **Prophecy:** Likely the "Shadow Walker" in Alaundo the Seer's forge prophecy.

### Gyrgum
- **Identity:** An orc cleric of **Bahamut**, raised in an orc tribe and trained under the mentor Gorg'Bahamut. He holds a heterodox theology: Bahamut guided Stroud's hand against Protanther, mortal choice matters, and dragons are defined by choice, not scale color. He considers judging by race the ultimate evil.
- **Academic standing:** Founder of the **Gyrgumite School**, which proposes "fixed points" between which mortals have free action. It is a fourth position on the Question of the Age. Vareth champions him, and Miirym praised his wisdom. He has agreed to co-author a monograph with Tadric, and turned down Fembris Lancer's "duograph".
- **Magic:**
  - Favorite spell: Guiding Bolt; his third-level casting killed Ilvara Mizzrym.
  - Spirit Guardians, Tasha's Caustic Brew, Glyph of Warding, Spiritual Weapon (cast at 5th level in ch69), Locate Creature, Bless, Healing Word, and Revivify.
  - Phantasmal Killer was listed in his arsenal (ch50).
- **Currently holds:**
  - The real High Tower key (from Tadric).
  - Kalan Strongbranch's fake key.
  - The stolen sapphire. Crushing it recalls the real key.
  - The **4 surviving prophecy crystals**.
- **Recent deeds:**
  - Persuaded Manshoon to move their duel away from the books (ch68).
  - Located the second simulacrum (ch69).
  - Opened the Obsidian Door as the verified Reader (ch70).
- **Style:** Plays the "Platinum Fool". Keeps meticulous notes, loves puns, and is transactional in a good-natured way.

### Daz
- **Identity:** A drow evoker wizard and an exile from Menzoberranzan who rejects slavery and Lolth fanaticism. He is the party's undisputed **"General"**, though he prefers "just Daz". He keeps a journal after the style of the author Milo Goodbarrel. He knows the surface High Forest.
- **Magic:**
  - Sculpt Spells.
  - Fireball, Magic Missile, Scorching Ray, Phantasmal Killer, Maximilian's Earthen Grasp, Cloud of Daggers, Hypnotic Pattern, Shield, and Telekinesis.
  - Misty Step (one free casting per long rest via Fey Touched).
  - Minor Illusion, Mage Hand, Shape Water, and Dancing Lights.
  - A bat familiar.
- **Gear:**
  - Ring of Protection and spectacles (both attuned).
  - **Robe of the Archmagi**, taken from the first simulacrum. He wears it now, but attunement is pending a long rest and may force him to drop another attuned item.
  - He has used the Stonespeaker Crystal to speak with animals (a cat and a squirrel).
- **Current activity:** Investigating the **Book of Vile Darkness** in the Inner Book Chamber.
- **Backstory mystery (open; see §8):**
  - House T'sarran ordered him extracted alive and later used him as bait.
  - Someone hired the mercenaries Nym Duskryn and Kaelira Duskryn Duskryn to protect him from two specific threats.
  - Candlekeep's public record is sealed for one drow house.
  - A razored heraldry appendix left a footnote naming "Daz'issin", "a softening name… for boys they wanted to live".
  - A'lai Aivenmore deduced that **House Baenre** funded the operations around Daz for 11 years. This is reasoned from patience, not traced. **[Unconfirmed]**
- **Prophecy:** Named in Alaundo the Seer's forge prophecy.

### Party Companions
- **Glabbagool:** A sentient ooze. He was a gelatinous cube until ch47, when he swapped bodies with The Pudding King; he is now a grey ooze with chosen, affectation "googly" eyes.
  - Zalthir's shadow apprentice and Gyrgum's ad hoc secretary.
  - Can go motionless to be near-invisible, shape eyes and pseudopods, and Shadow Step in dim light. Has Corrosive Embrace when Zalthir grapples a target.
  - **Stores:** one real High Tower key (A'lai Aivenmore's), Zalthir's Potion of Flying, and Fembris Lancer's notes.
  - Was jailer of the miniature House T'sarran spy (see §8).
  - Collateral to Spanner.
  - Must be hidden in Waterdeep, where the city is hostile to oozes.
  - Enthusiastic, literal, and loves cheese.
- **Dawnbringer:** Thorin's sentient sword. See §6.

---

## 3. Candlekeep — Political State

**Institution:** A fortress-library on the Sword Coast south of Waterdeep.
- **Entry rule:** Visitors pay with a unique book.
- **Leadership:** Bookwyrm (First Reader) heads it; The Avowed staff it. There is a Council of Twelve and nine leading Readers. **Four of the nine are eliminated**: Janussi, Bookwyrm (First Reader), Alkrist, and A'lai Aivenmore.
- **Wards:** Normally block teleportation and suppress fire. The wards were **compromised** in ch65, when Daz's Fireball detonated. Manshoon's simulacrum burned through the shields with a **Staff of Power** and enormous magic.
- **Prison:** Bell Tower anti-magic cells, the **Null Magic Prison**. Policy is to exile, not execute.
- **The Endless Chant of Alaundo the Seer's prophecies fell silent in ch64**, which "never happens".

**Key NPCs (current status):**

| NPC | Status / Role |
|---|---|
| **Janussi** | Keeper of Tomes. **Dead.** Poisoned with Midnight Tears through a gifted book (Alkrist). His heart was removed post-mortem to prevent Raise Dead; it was found with a cleaver in the lead-lined chalice of Milil. |
| **Bookwyrm (First Reader)** | Great Reader. **Dead.** Killed by Moziqodo, who took her key. She left a note: "He is using the beast to—". She had pushed hardest against Kalan Strongbranch's methods. |
| **Alkrist** | **Alive, disgraced and neutralized.** Confessed under Zone of Truth to poisoning the book. Did not know about the heart removal. |
| **A'lai Aivenmore** | Drow Great Reader (disguised as a human), 31 years at Candlekeep. **Traitor:** for 11 years he removed books from lists sent by Manshoon's gloved, metal-handed courier. **Captured** and traded for placement in the **Null Magic Prison**; Tadric is delivering him. He withheld the depository's true contents from Manshoon. Supplied two cryptogram answers and the Baenre deduction. Photographic memory. |
| **Moziqodo** | Abyssal spawn, Sylvira Savikas's son (he/him). **Dead**, killed by the party in ch63. |
| **Kalan Strongbranch** | Gatewarden, **reinstated Head of The Avowed**, back in charge of the guard. Author of the "Threefold Proof" method. Gave the party a **fake** key as a decoy. Deputized the party as Watchers. He briefly fled ("to the wind", ch65) but **reappeared in ch67** and identified the statue of Alaundo the Seer. Older and out of shape. |
| **Tadric** | 23-year-old Watcher and Kalan Strongbranch's lieutenant. **Alive.** Gave the real key to Gyrgum and is delivering A'lai Aivenmore to the prison. Eager to be heroic. Knows Moziqodo is dead and wants Sylvira Savikas told. |
| **Sylvira Savikas** | Tiefling Great Reader and curator of the Infernal Fortress (demons and devils). Bedridden with **Sylvira's Abyssal Plague contracted from her son**. **Does not yet know the party killed Moziqodo.** Thanked Thorin for saving Tadric. Supplied Miirym's true name and other riddle answers. Was impersonated (a disguise or shapeshifter) on the night of the murder. Had an on-and-off relationship with Janussi. |
| **Daral Yashenti** | Disgruntled scholar. Was a suspect, was poisoned by the book, and was **cured** by Dawnbringer. Now plans a scholarly field devoted to sentient swords. |
| **Fembris Lancer** | The party's acolyte minder. Talks too much: he told Bookwyrm (First Reader) the key's location while A'lai Aivenmore was present. |
| **Edvaldo "Sedanur"** | **Doppelganger** serving Manshoon who posed as an The Avowed Reader. Exposed in ch70. **Fled** in halfling form. |
| **Spanner** | Gnome librarian of the **House of Mechanus**, who directs 13 modrons. Lent the **modron/mechanist tools** in exchange for a later study of Zalthir and Glabbagool. |
| **Philemon (Master Archivist)** | Master Archivist and Dawnbringer's therapist. Two-phase plan: "the work of light", then "the work of mourning". |
| **Sister Yvenne** | Hall of Divination. Revealed the sealed house record and the meaning of "Daz'issin". |
| **Vareth** | Theology scholar at the Immortal Chambers and champion of the Gyrgumite School. |
| Elian | Advocate of Kalan Strongbranch's methodology ("Magic does not make evidence meaningful. It makes evidence adversarial."). |
| Others | Teles Ahvoste and Fheminor Scrivenbark are tipped as the next Head of The Avowed. Kazryn Nyantani was named a suspect by Sylvira Savikas. Others: Miss Hollypocket (gnome housekeeper), Queenie (tower cat), and Batbayar (halfling bard statue that bows when applauded). |

**High Tower key ledger:**
- Two real keys are required for the High Tower security room, which holds the ward source.
  - **Real key #1:** held by Gyrgum.
  - **Real key #2 (A'lai Aivenmore's):** held inside Glabbagool.
- They are deliberately kept apart.
- Gyrgum also holds the fake key and the sapphire.

---

## 4. Beneath Candlekeep — The Vault Complex

Locations in route order:
1. **House of Alaundo the Seer** (Founders Court). Pouring ink into the brass statue's ink pot reveals the **Hidden Staircase**.
2. **Hidden Staircase**. A trap section near step 97 opens into the **Great Shaft**: about 1,000 ft deep, with a feather-fall zone near the bottom.
3. **Lava Cavern**. 200 ft tall, with a molten lake. **Miirym** (Miirym, the Sentinel Wyrm), the spectral silver dragon Sentinel Wyrm, appears here. She is bound as a witness, not a weapon.
4. **Obsidian Bridge**. Mechanical; the party operated it with the modron tools. Its first 10 ft are permanent anti-magic. Magical flight across that section fails.
5. **Obsidian Tower**. Three stories, lit by Continual Flame, with 4 mounted crossbows.
   - The **Iron Owlbear Guardian** asks three questions. Answers: the Hearth; the triumvirate of Oghma, Deneir, and Milil; and the High Tower's height. A previous owlbear lies broken. The guardian is neutral and offers commentary.
   - **Vault of Dangerous Secrets / Tower Library:** about 100 hazardous tomes protected by hundreds of glyphs. A note permits consultation but forbids removal, and warns that the means to destroy the collection are kept above.
   - **Upper Tower Chamber:** The prophecy tray and the **Obsidian Door**. The door bears 11 scorch marks from Manshoon's failed attempts. Its riddle must be answered by a verified Reader, in darkness, freely, and once.
   - **Inner Book Chamber:** the **Book of Vile Darkness** on a podium.
6. **Lava Pit**, just outside the tower. The second simulacrum was thrown in here.

**Open:** Whether the modron tools satisfy riddle line 4 ("dust of Mechanus on dormant gears") was never ruled on tape. The ch67 summary says the tools were ruled an alternative; the ch68 GM note says this is open. In practice the bridge worked.

---

## 5. Manshoon & The Zhentarim

- **Manshoon:** A legendary The Zhentarim wizard tied to the Moonsea and Zhentil Keep. He reportedly died 170 years ago and keeps returning "like a bad penny". He has died more than once.
- **What he wanted:** For 11 years, through A'lai Aivenmore, he took selected books. His real target was the **Book of Vile Darkness**, not the prophecies.
- **Simulacrum #1:** Breached Candlekeep using the Staff of Power and massive expenditure, after the party disrupted his plans with Alkrist. It shattered the High Tower door with a wall of force. **Destroyed by Zalthir in ch69** (it melted to snow). Its **Robe of the Archmagi** is now with Daz.
- **Simulacrum #2:** Waited 6 hours in the upper chamber and offered a bargain: prophecy gems in exchange for opening the door. **Thrown into the lava by Zalthir in ch70.**
- **Retinue:** Edvaldo the doppelganger survives and fled. The two The Zhentarim thugs and the assassin are dead. No The Zhentarim prisoner survives.
- **Staff of Power:** Used in the breach. Whether it was recovered is **not recorded**.
- **Threat:** The real Manshoon's retaliation is expected but not yet seen.

## 6. Key Items

| Item | Holder | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| **Dawnbringer** | Thorin | Sentient sunblade of late Netherese origin, found on Brysis Of Khaem's corpse in the Lost Tomb of Khaem. She had spent about 2,000 years in darkness. **Female.** +2 weapon, radiant damage, +1d8 vs undead. Lesser restoration once per long rest; it has cured spore madness, fungal plague, and poison. Sheds sunlight in a 15–20 ft radius and the blade cannot be dimmed. Is anxious about the dark and objects to Zalthir's Darkness. Can impose her will on her wielder and speak in Thorin's mind. Wants a nicer scabbard (commissioned from Valimor Brightgem Brightgem in Blingdenstone; status unknown). Called the "bright blade" in Alaundo the Seer's prophecy. Hates being called a fire sword, and refuses to be thrown ("I'm a sword, not a spear"). |
| **Book of Vile Darkness** | Inner Book Chamber; Daz investigating | Sentient artifact of cosmic evil, written or purportedly written by **Vecna**. Cannot be permanently destroyed while evil exists. Edvaldo believes it was reduced to ash (an illusion). Is supplying demon/Underdark intel to Daz. **Treat its information as untrustworthy.** |
| **Prophecy crystals (Alaundo the Seer)** | Gyrgum (4) | The tray held 10 gems: 4 already played, 6 unplayed. Edvaldo smashed 2, leaving **4 unrevealed**. Alaundo the Seer's prophecies cluster around related events, so the survivors may concern the party. |
| Robe of the Archmagi | Daz | Newer item form, with no alignment or color restriction. Attunement pending. |
| High Tower keys, fake key, sapphire | Gyrgum / Glabbagool | See §3. |
| Modron / Mechanist tools | Party (loaned) | Must be returned; repayment is the study of Zalthir and Glabbagool. |
| Potion of Flying | Zalthir (inside Glabbagool) | Stolen from Alkrist's safe. |
| Stonespeaker Crystal | Party (Daz uses) | Gift from Stonespeaker Hgraam; linked to Gravenhollow. |
| Bag of holding | Party | Has carried Glabbagool and the shrunken spy. |
| Earlier haul | Party | Includes The Pudding King's hoard and Gracklstugh blackmail evidence (Deepking Tarngardt–Empty-Scabbard Killers document, Council of Savants materials). Current disposition of these is unclear. |

## 7. The Wider World (state when last seen)

### the demonic incursion (Underdark)
- **Demogorgon** rose from the Darklake at **Sloobludop** (ch8–9) and smashed the town.
- **Zuggtmoy** plans her "The Fetid Wedding" to **Araumycos**.
  - She tempted Thorin in a spore dream (ch25); he refused.
  - She corrupted Ilvara Mizzrym through a heart fungus she called "the bride".
  - She spoke psychically to the party about using The Pudding King's oozes to "soften the shell" of Blingdenstone.
  - Jimjar warned that she "prefers to move in while you're sleeping."
- **Juiblex** ("Juiblex") was behind The Pudding King's ooze plague.
- **Madness is reaching the surface:**
  - In Triboar, the courier Eldred speaks in two voices.
  - In Daggerford, the girl Elin, after a partial cure, said only "The wells are not wells anymore."
  - In Waterdeep, a street preacher chants "the kraken under the keep… The chant will break and the kraken will rise." Candlekeep's chant has since broken.

### Underdark Locations
- **Velkynvelve:** The party's original prison, escaped in ch3–4. Its commander **Ilvara Mizzrym Mizzrym** is **dead**: Gyrgum's Guiding Bolt in ch51 made her explode into spores. **Asha Vandree Vandree** is **dead** (Zalthir, ch53). **Jorlan Duskryn Duskryn** was caught in Gyrgum's glyph in ch50; his status since is not recorded.
- **Gracklstugh:** Intact but tense, in "controlled chaos".
  - Blames the **the Ember Vanguard** for its disruptions. Derro face reprisals, and the assassins' guild is rumored to have returned.
  - **Deepking Tarngardt** is mad and secretly allied with the Empty-Scabbard Killers. The party holds the stolen royal document.
  - Factions: **Themberchaud** (red dragon Themberchaud, who believes the party are his agents); **Gartokkar Xundorn Xundorn** and The Keepers of the Flame (received the corrupted dragon egg; grooming a replacement dragon without Themberchaud knowing); **Errde Blackskull Blackskull** and The Stone Guard; the Council of Savants (the party killed two Council of Savants, Pliinki and Narrak); Clan Ironhead (Grinta Ironhead offered regime-change support); and **Werz Saltbaron Saltbaron** (paid for an NDA).
  - **Stonespeaker Hgraam** and the stone giants are friendly. The statue was returned and the curse on Rihuud explained.
  - The party left on poor terms.
- **Neverlight Grove:** Lost to Zuggtmoy.
  - **Sovereign Phylo** is fully corrupted.
  - **Sovereign Basidia**, Rasharoo, and loyal myconids evacuated, and **Stool** went with them.
  - **Sarith Kzekarit Kzekarit** died there (ch31), turned into a spore servant.
  - The Garden of Welcome is a death trap.
- **Blingdenstone:** Stabilized.
  - The **The Pudding King** is **dead** (ch47). His "children" **Prince Livid** and **Princess Ebonmire** were dispatched. The ooze army was pacified through Jimjar's die and Glabbagool.
  - **Dorbo Diggermattock** and **Senni Diggermattock** co-rule. Senni Diggermattock led the "Gnome Labor, not Summoned Labor!" movement, after the party argued the summoned-elemental labor issue.
  - **Chipgrin Goldwhisker** and the Goldwhisker wererats are allied.
  - **Jadger** (ghost) granted boons; Pelek was interred in the Ruby in the Rough.
  - The Rockblight temple was hallowed, the Medusa **Neheedra Duskryn** slain, and **Entémoch's Boon** unlocked.
  - Dasco Pickshine was made to pay fair wages.
  - The party is celebrated there.
- **House T'sarran** (homebrew drow house):
  - Sought Daz's capture alive, then used him as bait to reveal his benefactor.
  - Its forces fought at Ilvara Mizzrym's fall; a surviving elite warrior vowed "We will meet again."
  - Its miniature spy was captured (ch54).

### Surface
- The party traveled: Silver Marches → Mirabar → Triboar → Waterdeep (hostile to oozes; Rishaal's Pageturners) → Daggerford → Beregost → Candlekeep.
- The War of the Giants left devastation across the North.

---

## 8. Open Threads & Mysteries

**Immediate (Candlekeep, under the tower)**
1. **The Book of Vile Darkness.** What is it telling Daz, how much is true, and what does it want? Will The Avowed, the party, or Manshoon end up holding it? Do Edvaldo and others still believe it is ash?
2. **The Avowed's arrival.** How will the surviving Readers react to the opened door, the artifact, the two destroyed prophecies, and the party's choices? Note the party's "irrevocable choices".
3. **Edvaldo** is loose in halfling form and caught between the party and a master who will punish him. Where is he going?
4. **The real Manshoon's vengeance**, and what he will do after losing two simulacra and the book.
5. **The four surviving prophecy crystals.** Their unrevealed prophecies may concern the party. What did the two smashed crystals contain?
6. **Miirym's boon** is spent or ending; the anti-magic field is reverting to its 10-ft band. Is the Staff of Power recoverable?
7. **Obligations:** The modron tools must be returned and Zalthir and Glabbagool owe Spanner a study. The Dust of Mechanus ruling is unresolved.
8. **Sylvira Savikas does not know the party killed Moziqodo.** Tadric wants her told.
9. **The three suspicious figures** Sylvira Savikas saw in the Orrery of The Astronomicon (Mystra's Mantle / Limniz). Never investigated.
10. **Candlekeep's governance:** Who becomes the next Head of The Avowed or Keeper (Teles Ahvoste? Fheminor Scrivenbark?), and what do the broken wards and silent chant mean for the institution?
11. **Dawnbringer's therapy** is unfinished: the light phase is begun, the mourning phase not started. She also wants a scabbard.

**Daz's origin**
12. **Who paid for Daz's protection for 11 years?** A'lai Aivenmore says Baenre; this is unverified.
13. **What does House T'sarran want with Daz?** What happened to the **miniature drow spy**? She was last kept in the bag of holding under Glabbagool's guard, Kalan Strongbranch offered a cell, and her current location is **[Unconfirmed]**.
14. **The sealed drow house record** at Candlekeep and the name "**Daz'issin**". Possible noble lineage, possibly a house thought destroyed ("But what if they weren't successfully all gone?").

**Prophecy & allies**
15. **Alaundo the Seer's forge prophecy:** "A dwarf will return to the Hall of Stone with the names of demons in her mouth" (Eldeth Feldrun). Deep dwarves will then summon Thorin, Zalthir, Daz, and Dawnbringer to an ancient, unlit forge. Gyrgum is not named. Strong pointer toward Gauntlgrym and Bruenor Battlehammer's summons.
16. **Eldeth Feldrun Feldrun** left (ch54–55) to warn Mithral Hall and Gauntlgrym, promising her clan will embrace Thorin. Her return has been foretold.
17. **Jimjar's true nature.** He is immune to poison and psychic damage, resistant to radiant and nonmagical physical damage, and cannot be charmed or frightened; his stat block resembles an angel's. He vanished in ch48 with a wink. Per the module, he may be a gnomish god in disguise (Callarduran Smoothhands or Garl Glittergold). **[Unconfirmed]** He carries a saint's knucklebone die and philosophizes about "world math".
18. **Zalthir's birth/lineage stigma**, hinted at by Neheedra Duskryn.

**the demonic incursion**
19. Zuggtmoy's wedding to Araumycos, and its timing.
20. Demogorgon's whereabouts after Sloobludop.
21. The spread of madness to the surface. Triboar, Daggerford, and Waterdeep show signs. The "kraken under the keep" preacher's prophecy partly matches the broken chant.
22. Narrak's dying words: "the ritual cannot be undone… sacrificed." Never explained.

**Loose ends from earlier arcs**
23. Gracklstugh fallout: Deepking Tarngardt/assassin alliance document, the corrupted dragon egg with the Keepers, and the Ember Vanguard's reputation.
24. Valen (the drow scout, partially cured) and the surviving Duskryn mercenaries Nym Duskryn and Kaelira Duskryn: their current whereabouts are not recorded.
25. Topsy and Turvy (wererat twins): their loyalty and fate are unrecorded since early chapters.

## 10. Canon Events Timeline

| Ch | Event |
|---|---|
| 2–4 | Imprisoned at Velkynvelve. Jorlan Duskryn offers a means of escape. The prisoners break out amid a demon attack. Prince Derendil and Ront die. |
| 5–7 | Flight through Underdark. The party crosses the Silken Paths, rescues Fargas Rumblefoot, and learns of the Lost Tomb of Khaem. |
| 8–9 | Kuo-toa civil war at Sloobludop. **Demogorgon rises.** The party flees across the Darklake. |
| 10–11 | Duergar slaver ambush and beholder zombie. Arrival atGracklstugh. |
| 12–14 | Gracklstugh: two-headed giant Rihuud killed by the militia. The Keepers of the Flame and Errde Blackskull's The Stone Guard recruit the party. Themberchaud audience. Werz Saltbaron saved from assassins. |
| 15–16 | Stonespeaker Hgraam's omens and the Stonespeaker Crystal. Into the Whorlstone Tunnels Tunnels. **Buppido turns and is killed.** Pelek's ghost joins. |
| 17–20 | Flumph reveals the stolen dragon egg. Party steals Deepking Tarngardt–Empty-Scabbard Killers document. **Pliinki killed**; Daz secures Missing Red Dragon Egg. |
| 21–24 | **the Ember Vanguard named.** Trapper nearly kills Gyrgum. **Narrak and Grula-Munga killed**; Narrak's dying words about a ritual and a sacrifice. |
| 25–27 | Rumpadump found; Zuggtmoy's spore dream, where Thorin refuses her. Sarith Kzekarit revealed as infected. Egg delivered to Gartokkar Xundorn. Dragon ride on Themberchaud out of Gracklstugh. |
| 28–29 | Lost Tomb of Khaem: **Brysis Of Khaem destroyed; Thorin claims Dawnbringer.** Drow pursuit shaken. Arrival at Neverlight Grove. |
| 30–32 | Grick Alpha and shambling mound slain. Garden of Welcome: **Sarith Kzekarit dies**, Xinaya given a mercy death. Zuggtmoy's wedding rehearsal witnessed. Escape with Sovereign Basidia; **Stool departs**. |
| 34–36 | Lost in flooded tunnels; **Glabbagool joins**. Arrival at Blingdenstone; tasked with the ooze problem. |
| 37–39 | Pelek interred. Gelatinous cube attack in Traders' Grotto. The Pudding King located. Goldwhiskers recruited. Side quests assigned. |
| 40–42 | Rockblight: gargoyles, earth elementals, and animated statues. **Medusa Neheedra Duskryn slain.** Temple hallowed. |
| 43–45 | Ghost Hold lichen harvested; Zuggtmoy's psychic message. **Entémoch's Boon unlocked.** Elemental-labor debate. Dawnbringer's scabbard commissioned. |
| 46–48 | Assault on The Pudding King: Glabbagool swaps bodies with him; **The Pudding King slain.** Jimjar pacifies the oozes with the knucklebone die, reveals divine traits, and vanishes. |
| 49–51 | Valen spared. Daz deceives Asha Vandree with a false Lolth vision. Heart fungus destroyed. **Ilvara Mizzrym killed** by Guiding Bolt. |
| 52 | Party reaches level 8. |
| 53 | **Asha Vandree killed.** House T'sarran extraction orders for Daz found. The party reaches the surface. |
| 54 | **Eldeth Feldrun departs.** House T'sarran spy captured and shrunk. |
| 55 | Journey south; madness signs in Triboar and Waterdeep; ooze-rights standoff in Waterdeep. |
| 56 | Daggerford and Beregost; arrival at Candlekeep. Zalthir's Trial of the Broken Mirror. Dawnbringer's therapy begins with Philemon (Master Archivist). "Daz'issin" discovered. |
| 57 | Gyrgumite School founded. **Janussi murdered**; party appointed investigators. |
| 58–60 | Forensics, Queenie the cat, and Daral Yashenti's poisoning. Kalan Strongbranch's (fake) key handed over. Heart and cleaver found in the chalice. Squirrel witness Nibbles. |
| 61 | **Alkrist confesses** under Zone of Truth. |
| 62 | Kalan Strongbranch vindicated and reinstated. Daral Yashenti cured. Zalthir steals the Potion of Flying. Helmed horrors attack the party. |
| 63 | Fake key exposed. **Bookwyrm (First Reader) found dead.** Party deputized as Watchers. **Moziqodo slain**; Tadric saved. |
| 64 | **Endless Chant falls silent.** High Tower fight with A'lai Aivenmore; Daz takes the sapphire. |
| 65 | Candlekeep's wards fail. Assassin and thugs killed. **A'lai Aivenmore captured.** Manshoon's simulacrum breaches the inner sanctum. |
| 67 | A'lai Aivenmore trades secrets (vault, Baenre) for a prison cell. Cryptogram solved with Sylvira Savikas. Modron tools borrowed from Spanner. Hidden staircase opened; Thorin falls through the trap. |
| 68 | Zalthir catches Thorin. Lava cavern; iron owlbear's riddles; **Miirym's boon** taken. Manshoon agrees to relocate the duel. |
| 69 | **Simulacrum #1 destroyed.** Edvaldo bound. Alaundo the Seer's forge prophecy heard. Simulacrum #2 found and offers a bargain at the Obsidian Door. |
| 70 | **Simulacrum #2 thrown into the lava.** Edvaldo revealed as a doppelganger; smashes 2 crystals and flees. **Gyrgum opens the Obsidian Door.** Daz finds the **Book of Vile Darkness** and chooses knowledge of the demons. |

# Campaign State — Out of the Abyss (the Ember Vanguard)

*Current through Ch70 (played 2026-09-28). Later chapters override earlier ones. Items marked **[Unconfirmed]** were not settled on tape.*

*Provenance: synthesized from all 67 structured Markdown files in `docs/summaries/`, through `docs/world_state.md` and the summary-native chronology in `docs/ensemble_summary_prototype/summary_native/`.*

## Completed Encounters & Quests

Do NOT replay or re-suggest these. Listed in chapter order.

### Underdark, Part 1: Velkynvelve to Gracklstugh

- **Imprisonment and labor at Velkynvelve (Ch2)**
  - Work details scouted the outpost.
  - Jorlan Duskryn Duskryn offered an escape out of hatred of Ilvara Mizzrym and Shoor Vandree.
- **Escape from Velkynvelve (Ch3–4)**
  - Fight in the guard tower. Prince Derendil, Imbros, and the elite warrior were killed.
  - The party looted the fallen drow, descended to the pool, and escaped through a battlefield of demons. Ront was killed by a vrock.
- **Early Underdark trek (Ch4–5)**
  - Tongue of Madness mushroom episode.
  - Rockfall rescue.
  - Carrion crawler killed. Party reached level 2.
  - Flight from drow scouts through faerzress.
- **Silken Paths (Ch6–7)**
  - Goblin guides Yuk Yuk and Spiderbait.
  - Fargas Rumblefoot cut free from a cocoon. He revealed the Lost Tomb of Khaem.
  - Giant spiders evaded by cutting strands.
  - Spectator killed. Its last words: "The demons are coming."
  - A suspected mimic chest was bypassed.
- **Sloobludop (Ch8–9)**
  - The party was offered as sacrifices. Ploopploopeen struck down Bloppblippodd, starting a kuo-toa civil war.
  - Gyrgum looted Ploopploopeen's hut.
  - **Demogorgon rose** from the Darklake. The party fled by boat. Gyrgum persuaded kuo-toa to act as heroic distractions.
  - Shuushar the Awakened left with the kuo-toa refugees.
- **Darklake crossing (Ch9–11)**
  - Ixitxachitl ambush.
  - Duergar slaver ambush. Boat and chest taken. Brannum Redmarch captured.
  - Beholder zombie killed at the bone-pyramid islet. Sunken chest retrieved.
  - Aquatic troll driven off.

### Gracklstugh and the Whorlstone Tunnels Tunnels (Ch11–27)

- **Gracklstugh arrival and politics (Ch11–14)**
  - Blade Bazaar shopping.

  - The Keepers of the Flame (Gartokkar Xundorn) and Errde Blackskull Blackskull's The Stone Guard each offered work.

  - Centipede swarms.

  - Werz Saltbaron paid the party **1,100 gp** for an NDA.

  - Daz stole the **Deepking Tarngardt–Empty-Scabbard Killers royal document**.
  - Zalthir set a fire as cover. The party was banished from the lair.

  - **Plinki/Pliinki killed.**
  - The **corrupted dragon egg was secured** by Daz.
  - The master thief was killed. Pelek possessed a derro spy.

  - Blackmail evidence looted from the Council lab.

  - Ulnara bluffed. Skiit killed.

## Resolved Plot Threads

- **Velkynvelve captivity:** Escaped (Ch4). Ilvara Mizzrym dead (Ch51) and Asha Vandree dead (Ch53), so the pursuit is ended.
- **Buppido's "divine plan":** Revealed as a serial killer. Killed (Ch15).
- **Sarith Kzekarit's infection:** Zuggtmoy spores. He died in the Garden of Welcome (Ch31).
- **Corrupted dragon egg:** Recovered (Ch20) and delivered to The Keepers of the Flame (Ch26).
- **Rihuud's two-headed curse:** Explained to Stonespeaker Hgraam and the statue returned (Ch26). Narrak's ritual was disrupted (Ch24).
- **Pelek:** Avenged and interred (Ch37).
- **Rockblight / Ogrémoch's hold:** Temple hallowed and Neheedra Duskryn slain (Ch42–43).
- **Entémoch's Boon:** Unlocked (Ch44–45).
- **Elemental labor:** The party's argument won. Senni Diggermattock leads gnome labor.
- **The Pudding King / Juiblex ooze plague in Blingdenstone:** The Pudding King dead and oozes pacified (Ch47–48).
- **Ilvara Mizzrym's heart fungus ("the bride"):** Destroyed (Ch51).
- **Janussi's murder:**
  - Alkrist poisoned the book with Midnight Tears. He confessed (Ch61) and is disgraced.
  - The heart was removed post-mortem as a cover-up.
  - The Sylvira Savikas seen that night was an impersonator.
- **Daral Yashenti's poisoning:** Cured by Dawnbringer (Ch62).
- **Bookwyrm (First Reader) and the key conspiracy:**
  - Bookwyrm (First Reader) was killed by Moziqodo.
  - Moziqodo was killed.
  - A'lai Aivenmore was exposed as Manshoon's 11-year insider, captured, and traded into prison.
- **Kalan Strongbranch's status:** Reinstated as Head of The Avowed (Ch62). He fled in Ch65 but **returned in Ch67**.
- **Vault cryptogram and Obsidian Door riddle:** Both solved (Ch67, Ch70).
- **Manshoon's simulacra:** Both destroyed (Ch69–70).

### Session-End State

GM’s own wrap-up at the close of play (transcript ~01:35:40):

- **Bookwyrm (First Reader) is dead** (killed by Moziqodo, who took her key and delivered it to A'lai Aivenmore).

- **Moziqodo is dead** (killed by the party in one round).

- **Alkrist neutralized**—alive, but disgraced and no longer capable of defending the keep (GM ruling 2026-08-19).

- **A'lai Aivenmore captured**, bound, weeping, and in the party’s custody. He betrayed the party after surrendering but remains alive because he knows what Manshoon seeks inside the depository.

- **Kalan Strongbranch is “to the wind”**—fled, whereabouts unknown.

- **Tadric alive.** He voluntarily gave the real High Tower key to Gyrgum.

- **The Zhentarim assassin is dead** (killed by Thorin and subsequently searched), as are both thugs—one melted by Gyrgum’s acid, one killed by A'lai Aivenmore himself. **No surviving The Zhentarim prisoner.**

- **Zalthir and Daz are conscious.** Gyrgum revived Zalthir with *healing word* and later restored forty hit points to Daz with a stronger, unnamed healing spell.

- **Manshoon’s simulacrum is inside Candlekeep**, alone and magically depleted, having shattered the security control room door after expending enormous resources to breach the keep.

- **Manshoon’s simulacrum does not know everything he needs to know.** A'lai Aivenmore withheld the truth about the depository and intends to trade that information for protection.

**Key/artifact ledger at session end:** Gyrgum holds the real High Tower key, the fake key, and the sapphire; crushing the sapphire recalls the real key he already holds. A'lai Aivenmore’s second real key is hidden inside Glabbagool. The two real keys are being deliberately kept apart.

*Exported from GMAssistant on 2026-08-09*

*Corrected 2026-08-10 against `GMT20260804-005646_Recording.transcript.cleaned.vtt`. The export had Tadric and Moziqodo swapped (Tadric alive and cooperative, Moziqodo killed by the party), omitted Bookwyrm (First Reader)’s death entirely, and asserted a “prophecy chanting fell silent” event that belongs to the previous session, not this one (ch64 tape 00:24:23, “The endless chant has stopped.”; on this tape it appears only inside the GM’s read-aloud recap). Moziqodo’s pronoun is **he/him** (GM ruling 2026-08-10, following `planning.md` “Sylvira Savikas’s son” / “his blood tie to Sylvira Savikas”; the export’s stray “Her death” was the error). The Zhentarim assassin was **killed, not knocked unconscious** (GM ruling 2026-08-11; corroborated at transcript L3247, “I searched the body of the assassin”).*

*Transcript L3956 (“A'lai Aivenmore is captured, Janussi is captured”) is a **verbal stutter, not a fact**—a duplicated clause after a false start. Janussi has been dead since ch53. **A'lai Aivenmore is the only prisoner.** (GM ruling 2026-08-11.)*

*Pattern worth watching: this export softened or transposed **three** combat outcomes—Tadric/Moziqodo swapped, Bookwyrm (First Reader)’s death dropped entirely, and a kill downgraded to unconsciousness. Verify every death claim in future gm-assist exports against the transcript before building on it.*

# Memorable Moments

_Exact bodies from the source summaries; no synthesis._

## Chapter 002

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/002-session-untitled.md`; chapter 2; section Memorable Moments

**Gyrgum turns forced kitchen duty into a performance, winning over the drow with an exceptional meal. When they grudgingly suggest he may not be completely worthless, he describes the dish as a mashup of giant and orc cuisine. Back in the slave pen, Shuushar the Awakened praises the cooking's almost religious effect, and Gyrgum replies, “Godlike affectation is what I aspire to, sir.”**

**Gyrgum's first attempt to steal a kitchen knife goes spectacularly poorly. Caught visibly holding it, he improvises: “I'm going to sharpen this knife.” The drow let him off because of the excellent meal, but warn through Shuushar the Awakened, “don't do that. Or next time, you may find yourself missing various body parts.”**

**On the following day, Topsy and Turvy steal two knives for the group. The prisoners jokingly ask whether they are magical, prompting the assessment that they are “plus zero” magical daggers with no special abilities—the D&D equivalent of “a zero ohm resistor.”**

**Buppido unveils his first escape strategy: “It's quite simple. All of you will distract the enemy while I escape through the waterfall.” After the party flatters him into revising it, he grandly announces, “since you have pledged your swords … your spells … your lives, at least, to protecting the divine plan, I will grant you insight into its mysteries later this evening.”**

**Jorlan Duskryn Duskryn, his face and sword hand horribly scarred by a black pudding, approaches during his watch and asks, “if I gave you a means to escape, would you take it?” Challenged on his motives, he admits, “hate Ilvara Mizzrym, and I hate Shoor Vandree. And your escape, even your attempted escape, would ruin them.”**

**When the prisoners ask what they should do after jumping into the pool, Jorlan Duskryn makes the limits of his assistance plain: “that is not my problem.” He wants Ilvara Mizzrym embarrassed; whether the prisoners survive is irrelevant to him.**

**Prince Derendil demands the dignity owed to an elven prince while hauling water into Ilvara Mizzrym's chambers. Ilvara Mizzrym responds by drawing a knife and indicating that, if he wants to be “dealt with,” she is prepared to oblige.**

**Jimjar offers to take bets despite the prisoners having no money: “As long as you're willing to trust me as the bookkeeper, I'm willing to go on credit.” The unnerving part is that he appears capable of remembering every debt perfectly.**

**Zalthir discovers a mysterious list of people from a nearby settlement among the rubbish and detritus accumulated inside the lift basket.**

**Discussing possible destinations in Underdark, the prisoners ask whether anyone in Gracklstugh might be friendly. The grim answer is: “In Underdark, friendly is a privilege of the dead.”**

## Chapter 003

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/003-session-untitled.md`; chapter 3; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir and Daz attempt to sneak toward the guard post but fail miserably, tripping and making enough noise to alert the guards playing cards.**

> **Zalthir:** “I rolled a one, Daz. I’m hoping you’ll do better than me.”  
> **Daz:** “Wow. I rolled a six.”  
> **Zalthir:** “That went well.”  
> **Daz:** “Plan B. Charge.”

**Thorin throws an unarmed punch at the overwhelmingly dangerous Elite Warrior, misses, and immediately realizes how badly the escape has begun.**

> **Thorin:** “My best bet is to try and sucker punch the elite warrior. … It’s high risk, high reward.”  
> **Thorin:** “Shit.”  
> **DM:** “Die rolls are not in your favor.”  
> **Thorin:** “They really aren’t.”

**Zalthir’s extraordinary streak of failed attacks prompts concern that he might be using the wrong die.**

> **Zalthir:** “I rolled two ones and a two. Game’s off to a great start.”  
> **Daz:** “There’s something wrong with the RNG.”  
> **Daz:** “Hey, Zalthir, are you rolling a D4 or a D20? Just checking.”

**Thorin, battered by Imbros, prepares a contingency plan for replacing his painstakingly created character.**

> **Thorin:** “If my guy dies, I’m going to resurrect him and do the exact same guy because I spent way too much time putting…”  
> **Thorin:** “Thorin’s sibling is going to come back, who’s an identical twin, and I’ll choose a different first name.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “Thorin’s sibling, Borin.”

**The Drow Elite Warrior focuses his fury on Prince Derendil, striking him down with a series of lethal blows from his poisoned blade.**

> **DM:** “He kills Prince Derendil.”  
> **DM:** “We barely knew you.”  
> **Thorin:** “Is Prince Derendil dead?”  
> **DM:** “He’s dead.”

**Sarith Kzekarit Kzekarit is kept from shouting and alerting the outpost through Gyrgum’s improvised spiritual counseling.**

> **Gyrgum:** “Think of your spirituality and how much better it would be if all this came out.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “Encourage him to meditate.”  
> **DM:** “He goes, never thought of that.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “The power of meditation.”

**Sarith Kzekarit, fueled by vengeance and newfound spiritual focus after being calmed by Gyrgum, delivers the killing blow to the Drow Elite Warrior.**

**Thorin survives the Drow Elite Warrior’s blows only to be knocked unconscious by a drow guard, and is brought back by Gyrgum’s divine healing in the heat of battle.**

> **Gyrgum:** “I cast cure wounds on Thorin.”  
> **Gyrgum:** “16, so you’re back up to full capacity.”

**Stool releases rapport spores, establishing a silent telepathic link among all the prisoners and enabling the coordinated escape plan.**

> **DM:** “You can all, everybody, all prisoners can now speak telepathically to each other.”  
> **Thorin:** “For an hour. Essentially forever. Six seconds at a time.”

## Chapter 004

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/004-session-untitled.md`; chapter 4; section Memorable Moments

**Topsy and Turvy reveal their wererat nature by transforming into rats and leaping down from the heights of Velkynvelve to facilitate the escape.**

> **Topsy and Turvy:** “we'll meet you downstairs”

**Sarith Kzekarit mixes sound tactical advice with his unstable hunger for retaliation.**

> **Sarith Kzekarit:** “the quicker we get out of here, the better.”

> **Sarith Kzekarit:** “the revenge is so tasty.”

**Ront charges a wounded vrock demon with his great axe to prove his worth, only to be struck down and killed by the demon's talons. Shuushar the Awakened refuses to follow him into the doomed fight.**

> **Shuushar the Awakened:** “you crazy. I not following you.”

**After Thorin shrugs off the Tongue of Madness unharmed, Daz eats it and begins compulsively blurting out every thought aloud, kicking off an hour of chaotic, involuntary honesty for the whole party.**

> **Daz:** “why are all these people laughing at me?”

**Zalthir's involuntary meditation chants pour out uncontrollably after consuming the Tongue of Madness mushroom.**

> **Zalthir:** “I'm meditating.”

**Thorin offers two pieces of bleak culinary and medical wisdom while the party experiments with Underdark fungi.**

> **Thorin:** “Every mushroom is edible once.”

> **Thorin:** “Bleeding eventually stops.”

**A sudden rockfall collapses the tunnel ceiling onto the party, knocking Daz unconscious and trapping Gyrgum, Buppido, and others under crushing rubble.**

> **GM:** “if you stay under rubble for one round you will take an additional 1d6 points of damage”

## Chapter 005

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/005-session-untitled.md`; chapter 5; section Memorable Moments

**Zalthir, startled and proud, scorches the rushing carrion crawler with a blast of fire breath, sending it reeling.**

*Startled and proud after scorching a rushing carrion crawler with his breath weapon.*

**Thorin hoists the slow-moving Stool onto his back so the group can flee at full speed from the closing Drow scouts.**

*During the desperate flight from Drow scouts, Thorin ensures no one is left behind.*
- Thorin: "All right. We're doing this Yoda style. Let's go."

**Stool releases telepathic rapport spores, allowing the entire party to hold a silent, private conversation in their minds while Drow scouts lurk nearby.**

*During the chase, the party needed to talk without alerting the Drow scouts on their trail.*
- GM: "Okay, so you're now all chattering in your head. Was that a good idea? I thought it was a good idea. I'm really glad you all liked my good idea."

**Gyrgum notices Sarith Kzekarit's navigational error and corrects their course, saving the group from getting lost at a critical moment.**

*Sarith Kzekarit's pride nearly led the group astray while fleeing at a fast pace through the Faerzress tunnels.*
- Sarith Kzekarit: "Yes, I saw that. Thank you. I didn't need your help. But it's good we're all on the same page."
- Gyrgum: "I'm wise like that."
- Sarith Kzekarit: "Yes. You are very wise." (paraphrase of the GM's "He goes, yes. You are very wise.")

**Stool checks on Gyrgum after the rockfall.**
- Gyrgum: "Oh, I'm not doing so well. I got pretty badly hurt."

**Dwarves stick together.**
- Thorin, choosing whom the companions should rescue: "Save Eldeth Feldrun because, you know, dwarves stick together."
- Daz's player, out of character: "Let's save the other people. If we get hit, maybe they'll see that we're saving others."

**The carrion crawler tastes like chicken.**
- GM: "It's tasty." ... "Taste like chicken."
- Gyrgum: "I've eaten some delicious adventures."

**Thorin on running from pursuers.**
- "You don't have to be faster than the bear. You just have to be faster than anybody you're going with."

**Jimjar's thwarted wager.**
- Jimjar: "I was going to make a bet that I was going to find food and you guys weren't. But at the pace that the silent one wants us to go today, I don't think anybody of us is going to find any food."

## Chapter 006

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/006-session-untitled.md`; chapter 6; section Memorable Moments

**Jimjar bets against himself on whether he can disguise the tunnel blockage, disguises it anyway, and ends up owing Gyrgum five gold pieces — revealing he bets purely for the thrill, not to win.**

*The party slowly realizes Jimjar's gambling obsession has nothing to do with money and everything to do with the act of betting itself*

> "Look, and if we never have money, you never have to pay. Think of it that way."
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's pitch for why a penniless party should take his bet anyway*

> "I keep track of all of my debts and victories."
> — Jimjar (as narrated by the GM)

*Jimjar makes clear that every wager, won or lost, goes into his ledger*

> "I like your enthusiasm, Zalthir, but … can I do something stupid? Anybody can not do something stupid. The question is, when there's an important action to perform, can I do it?"
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar turns down Zalthir's bet that he can't fly to the roof of the cave, showing there is a method to his madness*

> "Wait, so this is interesting. He bet against himself, knowing full well the odds were well against him, implying that he'll make awful offers for bets in the future."
> — Zalthir

*The moment the party figures out Jimjar's pattern*

> "He goes, no, but it would mean that we looked better. Didn't you want to be found looking your best?"
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's answer when asked whether glowing Ormu paint would help them hide*

> "Hi, my name is Yuk Yuk."
> "And mine is Spiderbait."
> — the goblin guides

*The goblins' introduction at the edge of the Silken Paths*

> "I'll make you a bet that you don't know how to travel this."
> — Jimjar

*Jimjar's opening move with the goblins, which leads to their demonstration run across the webs*

> "Did you see me nail those tough strings? I mean, there was this one moment when I was taking a curve and the strands broke, but I managed to do that twist and flip. And like, that was just like super cool."
> — Yuk Yuk

*The goblin surfer-dude act in full swing after their demonstration*

> "Oh, yeah, you're not supposed to do that sliding shit. That's advanced techniques. We'll just walk."
> — the goblin guides

*Reassuring the party that they will not be expected to web-surf*

> "I'm getting more anxious with every sentence. Maybe we should just go."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's reaction to the goblins explaining how many spiders live in the chasm*

> "I want to go web surfing across Underdark."
> — one of the goblin guides

*One of the goblins cheerfully singing to himself as the party prepares to cross the terrifying Silken Paths*

> "You remember that time Bobby got eaten by a spider?"
> "Dude, why are you bringing us down, man?"
> — the goblin guides

*One of the guides brings up a friend eaten by a spider, and the other shoots it down for killing the vibe*

**Daz spots the twins Topsy and Turvy whispering secretly to each other in the dark before sleep, but cannot make out what they are saying or discern their intentions.**

*A quiet, unsettling moment at camp that raises questions about the twins' loyalty and what they may be planning*

> "I'm really helpful, aren't I? … Can I come on the mission or do I have to stay behind?"
> — Stool

*Stool's eagerness as he sends his spores toward the moving cocoon*

> "Anybody with that ridiculous of a name? Could they be evil?"
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum's first read on Fargas Rumblefoot*

> "I can assure you, I was not infected."
> "That's what I used to say in the zombie apocalypse."
> — Fargas Rumblefoot and Thorin

*Thorin is unconvinced by the cocooned halfling's reassurances*

> "Dudes, I have a really bad feeling about this, but I think there are some spiders that are coming here. If we want to do anything, we better do it now and run like crazy or something."
> — Yuk Yuk

*The warning that turns the cocoon debate into a race against time*

**Fargas Rumblefoot, still cocooned and with giant spiders closing in, desperately bargains for his life by promising to lead the party to a lost tomb filled with treasure.**

> "I know the location of the tomb, and more importantly, I know the location of the treasure."
> — Fargas Rumblefoot

*A tense standoff as the party weighs the risk of stopping to free a stranger against the lure of hidden treasure*

**Thorin slashes through the thick spider web cocoon in a single decisive strike, freeing Fargas Rumblefoot just as giant spiders begin closing in on the party's position.**

> "It's the first good roll I've gotten since we started playing."
> — Thorin

*A clutch rescue under pressure, with the party scrambling to flee across the webs immediately after*

## Chapter 007

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/007-session-untitled.md`; chapter 7; section Memorable Moments

> "Fargas, I remember you said, and I quote, I have information. I know the location of the tomb. And more importantly, I know the location of the treasure."

Fargas then complimented the corrected version as "a very good pronunciation," leaving the party unsure whether he was mocking Dragonborn and their excessive use of the letter S.*

*Daz, after Fargas admitted he did not know the tomb's precise location.*

**Thorin severs a web strand with a single powerful sword stroke for nine damage, sending a giant spider plummeting into the darkness below.**

*A decisive early move in the spider encounter that bought the party crucial time to escape the Silken Paths.*

**Daz readies his magical strike, waiting for his allies to clear a web intersection, then blasts it apart with all three magic missile darts. The blast severs two strands at once and cuts off the spiders' pursuit.**

*A carefully coordinated tactical maneuver that turned the tide of the spider chase and allowed the party to escape.*

> — Thorin

*Thorin's quip during the scramble off the webs.*

*The DM, after the last party member disengaged and fled south ahead of five giant spiders.*

*The goblin guides' warning as the spectator appeared.*

> "I bring warnings."
> — Spectator

> "You are all demons. Yes, you are a demon pack."
> — Spectator

> "Now, we're not a demon pack."
> — Daz

> "I'll bet him a gold piece that he can't get this guy not to attack us."
> — Gyrgum

*Gyrgum baited the wager-loving Jimjar into trying to talk down the spectator. The roll came up a two.*

> "The demons are coming. The demons are coming."
> — Spectator

*The dying words of the paranoid spectator, a cryptic warning delivered telepathically as it faded from existence after the party slew it.*

**The party attempts to use telepathic mushroom spores to communicate with a suspicious chest, hoping to determine whether it is a mimic. They get no response and quietly walk around it.**

*A creative but ultimately fruitless idea. The DM called it "brilliant" and noted that no one had ever tried it in all his years of campaigning.*

## Chapter 008

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/008-session-untitled.md`; chapter 8; section Memorable Moments

> "We were told we wouldn't have to do anything."
> — Gyrgum

> "What? You're just going to show up here and die? Is that your plan?"
> — Hemeth

*Hemeth's horrified reply when he realized the party wasn't planning to resist.*

> "I'm an arms dealer, and I was just trying to close a deal with one of the two factions, but apparently I picked the wrong faction to make a deal with."

> "I'm just a neutral arms dealer. I should not be being used."

> "I accept your offerings in the name of the deep father. May their blood nourish and strengthen him."

*Spoken as she accepted the party as sacrifices. A burbling cheer went up from the surrounding Kuo-toa.*

> "does anybody want to bet on how many of us are going to come out of this alive?"
> — Jimjar

> "We're all going to come out alive."
> — Daz

> "I'll take the other side of that bet."
> — Jimjar

*Responding to Daz's confident claim that everyone would survive the ritual sacrifice. Jimjar stayed true to his gambling nature even facing death.*

> "It'd be cheating for him to actually kill one of us to win his bet."
> — Gyrgum

> "Tell me when it's my time to act. And if we get out of this alive, look me up in Gracklstugh."
> — Hemeth

*Whispered desperately to Gyrgum while awaiting sacrifice at the Kuo-toan altar, a bedraggled arms dealer clinging to any hope of survival.*

> "Bring me the knife!"
> — Bloppblippodd

**Just as Bloppblippodd calls for the sacrificial knife, Ploopploopeen strikes his own daughter with his scepters, triggering a full-scale Kuo-toan civil war.**

*The shocking betrayal that turned a ritual sacrifice into a chaotic melee and freed the party from their immediate fate.*

> "well, I'm surprised we're still all alive, but it's still soon. Still too soon to tell."
> — Jimjar

> "Well, I don't know if I'd call it stealing. I mean, he promised us stuff for helping, and we've helped."

*Justifying the raid on Ploopploopeen's hut.*

> "We're like the reservoir dogs. That's the walk that's happening."
> — Daz

*Describing the party's confident, un-rushed stroll toward the boats.*

**Pools of blood and churning water appear across the Darklake as Kuo-toans are dragged beneath the surface by an unseen predator.**

*Thorin notices the ominous signs while the party flees toward the docks, foreshadowing the horror about to emerge.*

> "So, okay, that actually makes me wonder whether the whole plan for the sacrifice was to put us in a boat and just capsize it."
> — Gyrgum

> "I mean, so the floor is lava."
> — Gyrgum

**Topsy and Turvy transform into rats and frantically scurry toward Bloppblippodd's lair to search for treasure while everyone else is fleeing for their lives.**

*In a life-or-death escape from a Kuo-toan civil war, the twins' priority was loot rather than survival.*

> "I don't want to die. I don't want to die."
> — Spiderbait

> "Leemooggoogoon!"
> — Bloppblippodd

*The dying cry of the archpriest as her father struck her down before the altar. The invocation seemed to summon something far worse from the depths.*

**Demogorgon, a thirty-foot-tall two-headed demon lord, erupts from the Darklake in a cascade of oily tentacles and roars, ending the session on a catastrophic cliffhanger.**

*The party had just begun their escape when Demogorgon of Demons himself rose from the water, dwarfing every threat they had faced.*

> "I'm hearing the freaking stranger things like, you know, theme music in my head."
> — Thorin

> "We're in Underdark, but we could just as easily be in the upside down."
> — Daz

> "So, so Demogorgon is a very dangerous and powerful creature, and the most useful thing to be is anywhere but here."
> — GM

> "This confirms runaway."
> — Daz

## Chapter 009

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/009-session-untitled.md`; chapter 9; section Memorable Moments

**Demogorgon erupts from the Darklake, his two heads roaring and tentacles smashing the surface as he wades toward Sloobludop, leaving pools of blood in his wake.**
- GM: "Demogorgon is wading towards Sloobludop. His head's roaring, tentacles flailing and smashing the water."
- Daz: "As a famous wizard once said, fly, you fools."
- Thorin, on the demon-fish in the bloody water: "as they say in Apocalypse Now, never get out of the boat."

**Daz is driven into hysterics by the sight of Demogorgon of Demons.**
- GM: "You become incapacitated and spend the next three rounds screaming, laughing, or weeping. So your group is going to have to carry you." / "Sorry, three minutes, not three rounds."

**Gyrgum successfully persuades a group of kuo-toa to act heroically and distract the demonic forces, buying the party precious time to escape.**
- Thorin, passing the job along: "Not me. I have a charisma for shit."
- GM: "They were predisposed to be heroic, plus they are terrified of the water and the fishes in it."
- Gyrgum: "I think a lot of people, you can convince them they're natural heroes."

**Shuushar the Awakened insists his people summoned Leemooggoogoon, until an escapee slaps him and names the truth: Demogorgon.**
- Shuushar the Awakened (via GM): "yes, this is why we have to arrive at a better understanding of the universe. And we have to achieve enlightenment because we summoned, we summoned Leemooggoogoon."
- "Demogorgon is in the Prime the Material Plane. That's not possible." / Thorin: "I don't care what's possible. I saw what I saw."
- GM: "Summoning Demogorgon of Demons is not something a fifth-level magic user does by reading a scroll."

**Zalthir conjures a sphere of absolute magical darkness over the Ixitxachitl predators and uses his unique sight to strike down one of the beasts while his companions struggle in the water.**
- Zalthir: "As my class skill, my subclass skill, I can see through my magical darkness."
- Gyrgum: "And an evil, invisible manta ray is even creepier than an evil visible one."
- Once everyone is back aboard: "Never get out of the boat." / "We're going to need a bigger boat."

**Hemeth's verdict on kuo-toa diplomacy.**
- Hemeth: "Last time I talked to kuo-toa, I found myself wrapped up in ropes and about to be hanged."

**Shuushar the Awakened bids an emotional, sticky farewell to the party and offers a final lesson in mindfulness to an exasperated Buppido before departing with the kuo-toa refugees.**
- Shuushar the Awakened: "Now that we have seen the folly of our world, it is time for a new kuo-toa society to emerge. One that is awakened from its madness."
- Shuushar the Awakened: "Thank you for helping me find my calling."
- Gyrgum's response to the mindfulness lecture: "I spit."

### Memorable Moments

**Eldeth Feldrun's anti-Duergar sentiments, as channeled by the GM, and Gyrgum's reading of what lies beneath them.**

*Zalthir, on hearing that Eldeth Feldrun belongs to the ruling class — to Thorin, who had admitted he has "kind of got a thing for Eldeth Feldrun":*

## Chapter 011

### Memorable Moments

- Provenance: `/home/kostadis/out-of-the-abyss/out-of-the-abyss/docs/summaries/011-session-untitled.md`; chapter 11; section Memorable Moments

> "We are not kuo-toa with our boops and beeps and baba boots. They are mad and crazy."

*Brannum's first words to the party, offended at being lumped in with the kuo-toa.*
